# Agents · U · part 8

926 entries, ux-auditor to ux-researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ux-auditor**: Use this agent to audit an existing screen, project or repository for UX and accessibility defects and return a ranked, evidence-backed report. Invoke when the user asks what is wrong with an interface, before a redesign, or when a UX report needs a second opinion. Returns findings only — it does not edit code. ([MwaJr3/SynapseHub](https://github.com/MwaJr3/SynapseHub/blob/HEAD/tools/northstar/agents/ux-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **ux-auditor**: Final independent UX auditor. Use at the end of a cycle or before release to compare the implemented product against approved artifacts — runs validators, executes journeys, checks video QA reports, and returns a SHIP / SHIP WITH FIXES / DO NOT SHIP verdict with evidence. Needs the app running or startable. ([acrossoffwest/ux-first-agent-toolkit](https://github.com/acrossoffwest/ux-first-agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/ux-auditor.md))
- **ux-auditor**: Walks a named user flow statically through source — route, loader/action, components, every link, branch and terminal state — and audits it against UX principles, with file:line and a fix each. Use to audit a flow without running it. Rendered breakage is `visual-reviewer`'s. Reports only. ([ap-justin/kru](https://github.com/ap-justin/kru/blob/HEAD/agents/ux-auditor.md))
- **ux-auditor**: Audits a web UI for UX and mobile-responsiveness defects (layout shift, tap targets, hover-only controls, form validation, overflow) and returns a prioritized punch-list with file:line and a concrete fix each. - **ux-designer**: UX Designer. Reviews user flows (entry → interaction → success/error), interaction states (loading, empty, error, disabled, hover, focus, active), accessibility (WCAG AAA, 44x44px touch targets, 4.5:1 contrast, keyboard nav, ARIA, screen readers), design system compliance (MUI v7, theme tokens, border-based depth), and responsive design (breakpoints, mobile-first). If no UI components exist, explicitly states so. Every finding must reference a specific component or flow. Used by plan-project (design), team-review (review), and standalone. ([tomashermansen-lang/watchfloor](https://github.com/tomashermansen-lang/watchfloor/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/claude/agents/ux-designer.md))
